Crate dependency DAG (no cycles enforced by workspace)

rasp-core   ←──────────────────────────────── (no deps on other rasp-* crates)
rasp-hook   ← rasp-core
rasp-context← rasp-core
rasp-taint  ← rasp-core, rasp-context
rasp-detect ← rasp-core, rasp-taint
rasp-policy ← rasp-core, rasp-detect
rasp-protect← rasp-core, rasp-hook, rasp-context, rasp-taint, rasp-detect, rasp-policy
rasp-telemetry← rasp-core, rasp-protect
rasp-cli    ← rasp-core, rasp-policy, rasp-telemetry
rasp-bench  ← rasp-core, rasp-protect, rasp-detect, rasp-taint
  • rasp-core has zero intra-workspace deps; all others import it.
  • rasp-protect is the only crate importing the full pipeline; no crate imports rasp-protect except rasp-bench and rasp-cli.
  • rasp-telemetry imports rasp-protect output types only; no reverse dep.
  • Cycle detection: cargo build fails on any cycle; CI enforces with cargo deny.

11. Core Schema and Core Types

All types live in rasp-core. Signatures are normative; implementations are non-normative.

11.1 SinkType and SinkArgs

pub enum SinkType {
    SqlQuery,     // SQL execution: raw query string + optional params
    Exec,         // OS command execution: argv[0..n]
    FileOpen,     // filesystem open: path + flags
    HttpRequest,  // outbound HTTP: method + url + headers
    Deserialize,  // deserialization: format + raw bytes
    LdapQuery,    // LDAP filter string + base DN
    XmlParse,     // XML parse: raw bytes (XXE surface)
    NoSqlQuery,   // NoSQL doc query: BSON/JSON doc bytes
    __NonExhaustive,  // allows adding sinks without major version bump
}

pub enum SinkArgs<'a> {
    SqlQuery  { query: &'a str, params: &'a [SqlParam<'a>] },
    Exec      { argv: &'a [&'a str], env: &'a [(&'a str, &'a str)] },
    FileOpen  { path: &'a str, flags: u32 },
    HttpRequest { method: &'a str, url: &'a str, headers: &'a [(&'a str, &'a str)] },
    Deserialize { format: DeserFormat, data: &'a [u8] },
    LdapQuery { filter: &'a str, base_dn: &'a str },
    XmlParse  { data: &'a [u8] },
    NoSqlQuery{ doc: &'a [u8] },
}
  • Ownership: borrowed lifetime 'a tied to hook call frame; zero-copy from app stack.
  • Mutation: immutable; RASP never mutates sink args (monitor purity invariant).
  • Serialization: not serialized at call time; copied into Evidence only on detection.
  • Compat: __NonExhaustive arm allows non-breaking additions; major version required for removal.
  • Failure mode: unrecognized SinkType variant → treated as Allow (fail-open); logged.
pub enum DeserFormat { Java, DotNet, Python, Json, MsgPack, Cbor, Unknown }
pub enum SqlParam<'a> { Str(&'a str), Int(i64), Float(f64), Bytes(&'a [u8]), Null }

11.2 HookEvent

pub struct HookEvent<'a> {
    pub sink:       SinkType,
    pub args:       SinkArgs<'a>,
    pub context_id: ContextId,       // opaque u64; correlates to RequestContext
    pub phase:      HookPhase,
    pub timestamp:  u64,             // nanoseconds since UNIX epoch (monotonic at hook)
}

pub enum HookPhase { Entry, Exit }
pub type ContextId = u64;
  • Ownership: stack-allocated per hook call; lifetime bounded to trampoline scope.
  • Concurrency: created on the calling thread; passed by shared ref into rasp-protect.
  • Not persisted; fields copied selectively into AttackEvent on detection.
  • context_id is the link to RequestContext; must be non-zero for a valid request.
  • Failure mode: context_id == 0 → anonymous event; analyzed but context-less; logged at WARN.

11.3 RequestContext

pub struct RequestContext {
    pub id:         ContextId,
    pub http:       Option<HttpInfo>,   // None for non-HTTP entry points
    pub taint_map:  TaintMap,
    pub call_stack: CallStack,
    pub started:    u64,               // ns since UNIX epoch at request entry
    pub correlation_id: CorrelationId, // propagated from incoming X-Correlation-ID or generated
}
  • Ownership: stored in thread_local! cell for sync apps; tokio::task_local! for async.
  • Mutation: &mut only within ContextGuard scope; never aliased across threads.
  • Concurrency: one RequestContext per thread/task; Context isolation invariant enforced by type system (not Send across task boundaries without explicit handoff).
  • Persistence: dropped at request exit via ContextGuard RAII; zero heap leak.
  • Serialization: not serialized; fields extracted into AttackEvent on detection.
  • Compat: additive fields only; all new fields Option<T>; version field for future migration.
  • Failure mode: context already active on entry → existing context evicted + WARN; should not occur in correctly instrumented app.
pub type CorrelationId = Arc<str>;   // shared ref; cheap clone for telemetry

11.4 HttpInfo

pub struct HttpInfo {
    pub method:      HttpMethod,          // GET, POST, …
    pub path:        Arc<str>,            // raw request path
    pub query:       Option<Arc<str>>,    // raw query string
    pub headers:     Arc<[(Arc<str>, Arc<str>)]>, // name-value pairs; immutable after capture
    pub body:        Option<Bytes>,       // buffered body; None if streaming / too large
    pub remote_addr: Option<IpAddr>,
    pub tls:         bool,
}
  • Ownership: Arc-wrapped fields; cheap clone into AttackEvent.
  • Body cap: MAX_BODY_CAPTURE_BYTES = 65536; excess bytes discarded (not tainted past cap).
  • Headers: canonicalized to lowercase ASCII at capture time.
  • Compat: new optional fields added without version bump.
pub enum HttpMethod { Get, Post, Put, Delete, Patch, Head, Options, Other(Arc<str>) }

11.5 TaintSource and TaintTag

pub enum TaintSource {
    HttpParam,    // URL query parameter value
    HttpHeader,   // request header value (excludes Host, Content-Type by default policy)
    HttpBody,     // POST/PUT body, URL-decoded or raw
    HttpPath,     // URL path segments
    Cookie,       // Cookie header parsed value
}

pub struct TaintTag {
    pub source:     TaintSource,
    pub byte_range: Range<usize>,  // byte offset within the original captured value
    pub param_name: Option<Arc<str>>, // e.g. "id", "q"; None for body blobs
}
  • Ownership: TaintTag is Copy-able (small struct); stored in SmallVec<[TaintTag; 2]> on TaintedValue.
  • Mutation: immutable after creation; propagation creates new TaintedValue with derived tags.
  • Serialization: serialized into TaintPath within Detection for evidence.

11.6 TaintedValue

pub struct TaintedValue {
    pub value:  Arc<[u8]>,             // the tainted byte sequence (possibly derived via concat etc.)
    pub tags:   SmallVec<[TaintTag; 2]>, // origin tags; multiple if from concat of two tainted inputs
    pub derived: bool,                 // true if produced by propagation op, false if direct source
}
  • Concurrency: Arc<[u8]> allows sharing across async tasks; tags cloned on derive.
  • Taint soundness: propagation ops that cannot be tracked (e.g., regex replace) set derived = true and retain all ancestor tags (conservative over-taint).
  • Budget: creation of TaintedValue increments CostBudget counter; at limit → BudgetExhausted logged, tracking stops (documented, not an error).

11.7 TaintMap

pub struct TaintMap {
    entries: HashMap<TaintKey, TaintedValue>,  // keyed by value identity
    budget:  CostBudget,
}

pub enum TaintKey {
    StrPtr(usize),    // raw pointer address of tracked &str
    HashKey(u64),     // xxhash of bytes when address is unreliable (e.g., format! result)
}
  • Capacity: MAX_TAINT_ENTRIES = 4096 per request; insertion beyond cap → oldest evicted + WARN.
  • Concurrency: not Send; owned by RequestContext; same thread/task only.
  • Failure mode: lookup miss → value treated as untainted (conservative for false-negatives; acceptable because missed propagation steps are documented).
pub struct CostBudget { remaining: u32 }   // initialized to MAX_PROPAGATION_OPS per request
pub const MAX_PROPAGATION_OPS: u32 = 8192;

11.8 Detection

pub struct Detection {
    pub rule_id:    RuleId,
    pub sink:       SinkType,
    pub severity:   Severity,
    pub confidence: Confidence,
    pub evidence:   Evidence,
    pub taint_path: TaintPath,
    pub ts:         u64,      // ns timestamp at detection
}

pub struct Evidence {
    pub raw_excerpt: Arc<str>,    // ≤256 bytes of the suspicious sink arg (sanitized for logs)
    pub matched_pattern: Option<Arc<str>>, // signature pattern id or None for semantic
    pub analyzer_signals: Vec<(Arc<str>, f32)>, // (signal_name, weight)
}

pub struct TaintPath {
    pub source: TaintSource,
    pub param_name: Option<Arc<str>>,
    pub ops: Vec<PropagationOp>,  // ordered list of propagation steps from source to sink
}

pub enum PropagationOp { Concat, Substring, Format, Decode, Unknown }
  • Ownership: Detection is Send + Sync; safe to ship to telemetry thread.
  • Serialization: serde::Serialize + serde::Deserialize; JSON-schema stable.
  • Compat: additive fields with #[serde(default)]; rule_id is string UUID stable across versions.

11.9 Action and Severity

pub enum Action { Allow, Block, Monitor }
pub enum Severity { Critical, High, Medium, Low, Informational }
pub enum Confidence { High, Medium, Low }
pub type RuleId = Arc<str>;   // UUID string; e.g. "sqli-001"
  • Action::Monitor: emit AttackEvent; do NOT block; do NOT alter response. Invariant: monitor purity.
  • Action::Block: abort sink operation; return RaspError::Blocked to calling crate.
  • Action::Allow: proceed; no event emitted (unless debug logging enabled).
  • Severity + Confidence: used in policy scoring; neither alone determines action (policy rule governs).

11.10 Rule and Policy

pub struct Rule {
    pub id:          RuleId,
    pub sink:        SinkType,
    pub kind:        RuleKind,
    pub action:      Action,    // action when this rule fires
    pub severity:    Severity,
    pub enabled:     bool,
    pub description: Arc<str>,
}

pub enum RuleKind {
    Signature { pattern_id: Arc<str> },   // named pattern in rasp-detect
    Semantic  { analyzer_id: Arc<str> },  // heuristic/scoring analyzer
}

pub struct Policy {
    pub mode:       PolicyMode,
    pub rules:      Vec<Rule>,          // ordered; first match wins
    pub waivers:    Vec<Waiver>,
    pub fail_mode:  FailMode,
    pub version:    u32,                // monotonically increasing; checked on reload
}

pub enum PolicyMode  { Monitor, Block }
pub enum FailMode    { Open, Closed }    // Open = allow on RASP internal error; Closed = block

pub struct Waiver {
    pub rule_id:    RuleId,
    pub path_glob:  Option<Arc<str>>,   // HTTP path glob; None = all paths
    pub expires_at: Option<u64>,        // ns UNIX timestamp; None = permanent
    pub reason:     Arc<str>,
}
  • Ownership: Policy is Arc-wrapped for hot-reload swap (ArcSwap); readers hold Arc<Policy>.
  • Hot-reload: PolicyEngine uses arc-swap::ArcSwap<Policy>; writer swaps atomically; readers see consistent version.
  • Compat: version field checked on load; downgrade rejected; additive fields allowed.
  • Failure mode: parse error on hot-reload → retain previous policy + ERROR log; never apply partial policy.

11.11 RaspError

pub enum RaspError {
    Blocked { rule_id: RuleId, detection: Box<Detection> }, // sink blocked; app must handle
    InternalError { msg: Arc<str>, source: Option<Arc<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>> },
    ContextNotFound { context_id: ContextId },    // hook fired outside tracked request
    BudgetExhausted { remaining: u32 },            // taint budget cap hit
    PolicyLoadError { path: Arc<str>, reason: Arc<str> },
    SinkNotRegistered { sink: SinkType },
}

pub type RaspResult<T> = Result<T, RaspError>;
  • RaspError::Blocked is the sole mechanism for blocking; the app translates to its own error type.
  • InternalError MUST NOT propagate as a panic across the trampoline boundary (host-safety invariant).
  • All variants implement std::error::Error; Display yields log-safe strings (no secret data).
  • Serialization: RaspError not serialized; Detection inside Blocked is serialized for telemetry.

11.12 AttackEvent (rasp-telemetry)

pub struct AttackEvent {
    pub event_id:      Uuid,
    pub ts:            u64,
    pub detection:     Detection,
    pub action_taken:  Action,
    pub request:       Option<Arc<HttpInfo>>,
    pub context_id:    ContextId,
    pub correlation_id: CorrelationId,
    pub agent_version: Arc<str>,
    pub host:          Arc<str>,
}
  • Ownership: Send + Sync; queued to telemetry channel (bounded mpsc).
  • Serialization: JSON (serde_json) for audit log; CEF string for SIEM.
  • Backpressure: channel full → event dropped + rasp_telemetry_dropped_total counter incremented; never blocks inline guard.
  • Compat: event_id is UUID v7 (time-ordered); schema version embedded in envelope.

11.13 RuntimeAdapter Trait (rasp-hook)

pub trait RuntimeAdapter: Send + Sync + 'static {
    fn runtime_name(&self)    -> &'static str;
    fn runtime_version(&self) -> &'static str;
    fn supported_sinks(&self) -> &'static [SinkType];
    fn on_init(&self, registry: &mut SinkRegistry) -> RaspResult<()>;
    fn on_shutdown(&self)     -> RaspResult<()>;
}
  • Implementors: one per target runtime (JVM bridge, Node.js native module, .NET P/Invoke, etc.).
  • on_init: registers hook handlers with SinkRegistry; called once at agent startup.
  • supported_sinks: declares which sinks this adapter instruments; others short-circuit.
  • Concurrency: Send + Sync required; adapter may be called from multiple threads.
  • Failure mode: on_init failure → RaspError::InternalError; agent startup aborts (fail-closed).

11.14 SinkRegistry (rasp-hook)

pub struct SinkRegistry {
    handlers: HashMap<SinkType, Box<dyn SinkHandler>>,
}

pub trait SinkHandler: Send + Sync {
    fn on_entry(&self, event: &HookEvent<'_>) -> RaspResult<Action>;
    fn on_exit (&self, event: &HookEvent<'_>, action_taken: Action);
}
  • Ownership: SinkRegistry is Arc-wrapped; shared across threads after init; immutable post-init.
  • Registration: mutable only during RuntimeAdapter::on_init; read-only thereafter.
  • Dispatch: on_entry called synchronously in the protected app's call frame; must complete within overhead budget.
  • Failure mode: no handler for SinkType → Action::Allow + SinkNotRegistered logged at DEBUG.

11.15 ContextGuard (rasp-context)

pub struct ContextGuard {
    _priv: (),   // non-constructable outside rasp-context
}
impl ContextGuard {
    pub fn enter(ctx: RequestContext) -> Self;  // installs ctx into thread/task-local
    pub fn current() -> Option<&'static RequestContext>; // borrow current; None if no active request
    pub fn current_mut() -> Option<RefMut<'static, RequestContext>>;
}
impl Drop for ContextGuard {
    fn drop(&mut self); // removes context from local store; zeroes taint_map
}
  • RAII: context is guaranteed removed on drop even if the request handler panics (catch_unwind boundary in trampoline).
  • Concurrency: ContextGuard is !Send; cannot be moved to another thread.
  • Failure mode: Drop panics are suppressed (catch any secondary panic); WARN logged.

11.16 InlineGuard (rasp-protect)

pub struct InlineGuard {
    registry: Arc<SinkRegistry>,
    policy:   Arc<ArcSwap<Policy>>,
    telemetry: TelemetrySender,
}
impl InlineGuard {
    pub fn on_sink_entry<'a>(&self, event: &HookEvent<'a>) -> Action;
    // returns Action to caller (trampoline); Block => caller aborts sink op
}
  • Hot path: on_sink_entry runs in-line in every sink call; must respect overhead budget.
  • Short-circuit: if no RequestContext active OR sink args are untainted → returns Allow immediately.
  • Panic safety: std::panic::catch_unwind wraps the full pipeline; on panic → fail-mode action returned; InternalError logged.
  • Concurrency: &self only; all mutable state inside RequestContext (per-request, not shared).

11.17 TelemetrySender (rasp-telemetry)

pub struct TelemetrySender {
    tx: tokio::sync::mpsc::Sender<AttackEvent>,
}
impl TelemetrySender {
    pub fn send(&self, event: AttackEvent);  // non-blocking; drops on full channel
    pub fn flush_sync(&self, timeout: Duration) -> bool; // blocking drain; used at shutdown
}
  • Non-blocking send: try_send; on Err(Full) → drop + increment rasp_telemetry_dropped_total.
  • Channel capacity: configurable; default 8192 events; backpressure never propagates to guard.
  • flush_sync: called during graceful shutdown; returns false if timeout exceeded (events lost; logged).

11.18 PolicyEngine (rasp-policy)

pub struct PolicyEngine {
    policy: ArcSwap<Policy>,
}
impl PolicyEngine {
    pub fn evaluate(&self, detection: &Detection, http_path: Option<&str>) -> Action;
    pub fn reload(&self, new_policy: Policy) -> RaspResult<()>;
    pub fn current_version(&self) -> u32;
}
  • evaluate: reads Arc<Policy> snapshot; applies mode + first-match rule + waiver; deterministic.
  • reload: validates new_policy.version > current; swaps atomically; old Arc dropped when readers release.
  • Compat: reload rejects downgrade (version must increase); returns PolicyLoadError otherwise.
  • Concurrency: ArcSwap provides wait-free reads; write lock only during reload.

11.19 Serialization and Compatibility Guarantees

Type serde? Schema stability Breaking change policy
SinkType yes stable; __NonExhaustive arm new arms = minor; removal = major
HookEvent no internal; not persisted any change allowed within major
RequestContext no internal any change allowed within major
Detection yes stable JSON schema; versioned new optional fields = minor; field removal = major
AttackEvent yes (JSON + CEF) stable; CEF extension fields versioned additive = minor
Policy yes (TOML + JSON) stable; version field guards reload additive = minor; semantic change = major
Rule / RuleKind yes stable new RuleKind arms = minor
RaspError no internal any change within major
TaintTag yes (for evidence) stable in TaintPath serialization additive = minor
  • All stable types use #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)] for deserialization to catch schema drift early.
  • #[serde(default)] on optional new fields ensures forward compatibility of persisted events.
  • rasp-core version governs all stable types; single crate semver governs entire ABI contract.

12. Phase 0 — Skeleton — crates, core types, a mock instrumented app that calls the hook ABI for ONE sink (SQL exec); engine logs the HookEvent (no detection)

0.1 Objectives

  • Establish all 10 workspace crates (rasp-core, rasp-hook, rasp-context, rasp-taint, rasp-detect, rasp-policy, rasp-protect, rasp-telemetry, rasp-cli, rasp-bench) with correct Cargo.toml dependencies; CI compiles clean.
  • Define every locked canonical core type exactly as specified (see section 11): SinkType, HookEvent, CallContext, RequestContext, TaintSource, TaintTag, TaintedValue, Detection, Action, Rule, Policy, Severity, RaspError — all in rasp-core.
  • Wire ONE hook call path: mock app → hook ABI entry (rasp-hook) → rasp-protect dispatch → rasp-telemetry log. No taint, no detection, no policy enforcement.
  • Establish host-safety invariant structurally: hook entry is catch_unwind-wrapped; any RaspError propagates to telemetry, not to the host.
  • Constraints introduced: no_std-compatible core types where feasible; #[must_use] on Action; all public APIs pub(crate)-first then selectively pub.

0.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

  • Workspace (Cargo.toml root): [workspace] members = ["rasp-core","rasp-hook","rasp-context","rasp-taint","rasp-detect","rasp-policy","rasp-protect","rasp-telemetry","rasp-cli","rasp-bench"]; resolver = "2"; edition = "2021"; rust-version = "1.78"; shared [workspace.dependencies]: serde={version="1",features=["derive"]}, thiserror="1", tracing="0.1", tracing-subscriber="0.3", uuid={version="1",features=["v4"]}, bytes="1", smallvec="1".
  • rasp-core/src/lib.rs (re-exports all canonical types): types/sink.rs — SinkType enum + SinkArgs (variant-per-sink, Arc<str>/Bytes payload); types/hook.rs — HookEvent { sink: SinkType, args: SinkArgs, context_id: Uuid, phase: Phase, timestamp: u64 }; types/context.rs — RequestContext, HttpInfo, CallStack, CorrelationId = Uuid; types/taint.rs — TaintSource, TaintTag, TaintedValue, TaintMap = HashMap<CorrelationId, Vec<TaintedValue>>; types/detect.rs — Detection, Evidence, TaintPath, Severity, Confidence; types/action.rs — Action (#[must_use]; impl Default for Action { Allow }); types/policy.rs — Rule, Policy, FailMode, Waiver; types/error.rs — RaspError via thiserror (HookPanic, ContextMissing, TaintBudgetExceeded, DetectionError, PolicyError, TelemetryError, InternalError(String)). Type design locked by ADR-001 (Rust language choice) and follows B7 (Rust for Rustaceans, Ch. 4 type design).
  • rasp-hook/src/lib.rs (hook ABI + dispatch): pub trait SinkHook: Send + Sync { fn on_entry(&self, event: &HookEvent) -> Result<Action, RaspError>; fn on_exit(&self, event: &HookEvent); }; HookRegistry: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<SinkType, Arc<dyn SinkHook>>>> (registration at startup only, read-heavy); hook_entry(sink, args, ctx_id) -> Action constructs HookEvent, looks up registry, calls on_entry inside std::panic::catch_unwind, on panic → telemetry::record_guard_panic + policy::fail_mode_action(); mod trampoline reserved stub (#[allow(dead_code)]); mod adapter: pub trait RuntimeAdapter: Send + Sync { fn name(&self) -> &'static str; fn register_sinks(&self, registry: &mut HookRegistry); } stub.
  • rasp-protect/src/lib.rs (inline guard orchestrator, Phase 0 passthrough): pub struct RaspGuard { registry: Arc<HookRegistry>, ... } singleton built once; impl SinkHook for RaspGuard { fn on_entry(...) { log HookEvent via tracing::info!; Ok(Action::Allow) } fn on_exit(...) {} }.
  • rasp-telemetry/src/lib.rs (Phase 0 minimal): pub fn record_hook_event(e: &HookEvent) → tracing::info!(event=?e, "hook_event"); pub fn record_guard_panic(sink: &SinkType, msg: &str) → tracing::error!; metrics mod stub: pub fn increment_hook_calls(sink: &SinkType) (no-op).
  • rasp-cli/src/main.rs (mock instrumented app): initializes tracing_subscriber::fmt logger; builds RaspGuard, registers it in HookRegistry for SinkType::SqlQuery; simulates 3 SQL calls hook_entry(SqlQuery, SinkArgs::Sql("SELECT 1"), ctx_id); asserts returned Action::Allow for each; prints [RASP] HookEvent logged to stdout (operator-visible confirmation).
  • Test harness: rasp-core/tests/types_round_trip.rs — serde_json round-trip all types.

0.3 Deliverable

  • cargo build --workspace succeeds; zero warnings on stable 1.78.
  • cargo run -p rasp-cli prints 3 hook_event tracing lines + [RASP] HookEvent logged.
  • cargo test --workspace passes: type round-trips, Action::Allow default, HookRegistry register+lookup.
  • Operators can inspect: structured JSON tracing output (configurable via RUST_LOG).

0.4 Acceptance Criteria

Criterion Target
cargo build --workspace 0 errors, 0 warnings
cargo test --workspace 100% pass (≥8 tests)
hook_entry for SQL on mock app returns Action::Allow, logs event
catch_unwind on hook panic returns Action::Allow (fail-open default), logs record_guard_panic
Core type serde round-trip all 13 types serialize/deserialize without loss
Binary size (rasp-cli debug) < 15 MB
Stack depth in hook_entry ≤ 5 frames from call site to log (verifiable via RUST_BACKTRACE=1)

0.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Purpose Expected Output
rasp-core/tests/fixtures/hook_event_sql.json canonical HookEvent{SqlQuery} JSON round-trip identity
rasp-core/tests/fixtures/hook_event_exec.json future sink placeholder; deserialization must not panic Ok(HookEvent)
rasp-core/tests/fixtures/policy_default.json Policy{mode:Monitor, fail_mode:Open} round-trip identity
rasp-core/tests/fixtures/malformed_hook.json missing context_id field Err(serde_json::Error), no panic
rasp-hook/tests/fixtures/registry_empty.txt calling hook_entry on unregistered sink Action::Allow, log line "no_hook_registered"

0.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root Cause Debug Signal Recovery
catch_unwind missing on hook path host process crashes on guard bug SinkHook::on_entry panics without wrapper no log; process aborts add panic::catch_unwind in hook_entry; CI test that injects panic
Arc<RwLock<HookRegistry>> write-lock at request time latency spike under load registry mutated after init lock contention metric; tracing::warn! on write freeze registry after startup; use ArcSwap or OnceLock
SinkArgs carrying Arc<str> cloned per hook call excess allocation on hot path naive clone in HookEvent ctor allocation profiler (DHAT) use Cow<'_, str> or pass by reference until taint phase needs ownership
uuid::Uuid::new_v4() called in hot path CSPRNG syscall overhead random source init per call perf stat getrandom pre-generate per-request ID in rasp-context; pass into hook_entry
tracing::info! formatted unconditionally log overhead in prod format string evaluated before level check disabled-level latency benchmark use tracing lazy format + check level; gate on event_enabled!
Circular crate dependency build fails rasp-protect depends on rasp-hook which depends on rasp-protect cargo build cycle error strict DAG: rasp-protect depends on rasp-hook; rasp-hook depends only on rasp-core
#[must_use] on Action ignored in test stubs blocking never enforced test author discards return value rustc warning → CI error #![deny(unused_must_use)] in workspace Cargo.toml

0.7 Performance Concerns

  • HookRegistry lookup: HashMap<SinkType, Arc<dyn SinkHook>> — SinkType is small enum, hash is O(1); read via RwLock::read() — low contention if registry frozen post-init.
  • HookEvent construction: stack-allocated minus Arc<str> in SinkArgs — avoid heap if SinkArgs::Sql borrows from caller frame (explore Cow).
  • catch_unwind overhead: ~3 ns on non-panic path (single branch + TLS check); acceptable.
  • tracing::info! in Phase 0: ~50–200 ns per call with subscriber; must be gated by level filter in benchmark builds.
  • Uuid::new_v4() — getrandom syscall: 100–500 ns; amortize by generating once per request boundary in rasp-context, not per sink call.
  • Phase 0 does NOT establish latency budget enforcement (that's Phase 7); this phase sets the baseline measurement.

0.8 Edge Cases

  • hook_entry called with context_id = Uuid::nil() → must not panic; telemetry logs "nil_context_id".
  • SinkArgs::Sql with empty string ("") → HookEvent constructed normally; Action::Allow.
  • SinkArgs::Sql with 1 MB payload → Arc<str> allocates; no truncation in Phase 0; Phase 3 introduces bounds.
  • HookRegistry lookup for unregistered SinkType → Action::Allow + tracing::warn!("no_hook_registered").
  • Concurrent hook_entry calls from multiple threads before RwLock settled → no deadlock (read-only after init).
  • catch_unwind on AssertUnwindSafe-wrapped closure — double-panic (panic in panic handler) → process aborts; acceptable (unrecoverable).
  • serde round-trip with phase: Phase::Exit + missing timestamp → Err; no silent default injection.

0.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Containment Recovery Operator Signal
Guard panic in on_entry catch_unwind catches; record_guard_panic logged single request; hook returns Allow (fail-open) isolated per call fix guard bug, redeploy tracing::error! + rasp_guard_panics_total counter
Registry deadlock (write after init) lock timeout (none in Phase 0); latency spike all concurrent requests blocked freeze registry post-startup redesign to OnceLock<HookRegistry> lock-wait histogram > threshold
Crate DAG cycle build failure CI only; no runtime revert dependency edge enforce DAG in CI cargo build error output
Uuid::nil() context ID telemetry log anomaly single event; no crash log + allow trace to missing context init nil_context_id counter
RaspError::InternalError returned logged; Action::Allow returned single request fail-open diagnose via event detail rasp_internal_errors_total counter

0.10 Operational Notes

  • Logging: RUST_LOG=rasp=info for structured JSON; RUST_LOG=rasp=trace for per-hook debug.
  • Metrics stubs: rasp_hook_calls_total{sink}, rasp_guard_panics_total — counters only; no export in Phase 0.
  • Deployment: rasp-cli binary is standalone; no daemon; restarted to pick up crate changes.
  • Rollback: swap binary; no persistent state in Phase 0.
  • Compatibility: all crate public APIs are #[non_exhaustive] on enums; additive-only changes permitted without semver bump within Phase 0.
  • CI gates: cargo clippy --workspace -- -D warnings; cargo fmt --check; cargo test --workspace.

13. Phase 1 — Hook / instrumentation framework — hook ABI (sink entry/exit), registration, runtime-adapter interface, safe trampolines, multi-sink dispatch

1.1 Objectives

  • Complete rasp-hook into a production-grade hook framework: full SinkHook ABI (entry + exit), per-sink HookRegistration metadata, thread-safe frozen registry, RuntimeAdapter interface for per-runtime instrumentation, safe trampoline stubs, and multi-sink dispatch with per-sink dispatch metrics.
  • Support all 8 SinkType variants for dispatch (not all analyzers implemented yet — downstream phases add them).
  • Enforce host-safety invariant on EVERY sink path: catch_unwind on both on_entry and on_exit; bounded execution time (soft wall-clock check; hard timeout deferred to Phase 7).
  • Introduce HookRegistration { sink: SinkType, handler: Arc<dyn SinkHook>, priority: u8, enabled: AtomicBool } — allows per-sink runtime enable/disable without registry rebuild.
  • Constraints: no async in hook_entry/hook_exit — ABI must be callable from sync contexts (incl. FFI callbacks from runtime adapter); async wrappers are in rasp-context.

1.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

  • rasp-hook/src/registry.rs: pub struct HookRegistry { inner: Arc<RegistryInner> } frozen after freeze(); RegistryInner: sinks: [Option<HookRegistration>; SINK_COUNT] array-indexed by SinkType as usize, frozen: AtomicBool; register(sink, handler, priority) → Err(RaspError::RegistryFrozen) if frozen; freeze() sets frozen, subsequent reads take no lock (array immutable post-freeze); lookup(sink) -> Option<&HookRegistration> zero-lock after freeze, SINK_COUNT = 16 (current + reserved); HookRegistration { sink, handler: Arc<dyn SinkHook>, priority: u8, enabled: AtomicBool }.
  • rasp-hook/src/dispatch.rs: hook_entry(reg, sink, args, ctx_id) -> Action — (1) reg.lookup(sink)→None⇒Allow+warn; (2) enabled.load(Relaxed)==false⇒Allow (silent skip); (3) build HookEvent{sink, args, context_id: ctx_id, phase: Phase::Entry, timestamp: now_ns()}; (4) catch_unwind(AssertUnwindSafe(|| handler.on_entry(&event))): Ok(Ok(action))⇒incr hook_calls_total{sink,outcome=action}+return action, Ok(Err(e))⇒log+return fail_mode_action(), Err(panic)⇒record_guard_panic+return fail_mode_action(). hook_exit(reg, sink, args, ctx_id): same lookup, catch_unwind on on_exit, errors logged, no action returned. now_ns() -> u64: SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap_or_default().as_nanos() as u64. fail_mode_action() -> Action: reads global FAIL_MODE: OnceLock<FailMode>; FailMode::Open→Allow, FailMode::Closed→Block.
  • rasp-hook/src/trampoline.rs: reserved module; pub struct Trampoline { original_fn: *mut u8, hook_fn: *mut u8 } (pointer types, unsafe constructor); impl Trampoline { pub unsafe fn install(&self) -> Result<(), RaspError> stub, returns Err(RaspError::NotImplemented) in Phase 1; establishes the type for per-runtime adapters to fill, avoiding an API break in Phase 5+ (native adapter); #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]-gated (platform-specific). Trampoline shape follows P13 (Luk et al., Pin — trampoline + JIT interception model) and is locked by ADR-020 (C-ABI hook target).
  • rasp-hook/src/adapter.rs: pub trait RuntimeAdapter: Send + Sync { fn name(&self) -> &'static str; fn runtime_version(&self) -> semver::Version; fn register_sinks(&self, registry: &mut HookRegistry) -> Result<(), RaspError>; fn on_attach(&self) -> Result<(), RaspError>; fn on_detach(&self) -> Result<(), RaspError>; } (on_attach runs once at RASP init, on_detach cleanup and must not panic); pub struct MockAdapter test implementation registers a MockSinkHook for SqlQuery (used in Phase 1 tests); adapter list stored in RaspGuard::adapters: Vec<Box<dyn RuntimeAdapter>>, iterated at startup only.
  • rasp-hook/src/abi.rs — C-compatible ABI surface (FFI boundary for future native adapters):
#[no_mangle] pub extern "C" fn rasp_hook_entry(sink_id: u32, arg_ptr: *const u8, arg_len: usize, ctx_id: *const u8) -> u32

sink_id→SinkType::from_u32 (fallible; unknown → Allow); arg_ptr/arg_len→SinkArgs::Raw(Bytes::copy_from_slice(...)); ctx_id→16-byte UUID; returns 0=Allow, 1=Block; full unsafe block, pointer validity checked (null → Allow + warn).

  • rasp-protect/src/lib.rs update: RaspGuard::new(adapters, fail_mode, telemetry) runs adapter.on_attach(), registers sinks, freezes registry; impl SinkHook for RaspGuard calls rasp-context to resolve RequestContext (stub in Phase 1), calls rasp-taint/rasp-detect/rasp-policy (stubs), returns Action.
  • Tests: rasp-hook/tests/dispatch_test.rs (register MockAdapter, freeze, call hook_entry for each SinkType, assert Action::Allow, check hook_calls_total); rasp-hook/tests/panic_safety_test.rs (register panicking hook, assert no panic propagates, guard_panics_total incremented); rasp-hook/tests/registry_freeze_test.rs (register after freeze → Err(RegistryFrozen)); rasp-hook/tests/abi_ffi_test.rs (call rasp_hook_entry with valid/null/oversized args, assert no UB, correct return).

1.3 Deliverable

  • rasp-hook compiles with all 8 sink dispatch paths.
  • MockAdapter registers SqlQuery hook; hook_entry dispatches correctly; exit hook fires.
  • rasp_hook_entry C ABI callable via dlsym in integration test; returns 0 for allow.
  • Registry freeze prevents mutation; AtomicBool enable/disable toggleable at runtime.
  • All panic-safety tests pass; fail_mode_action() returns Allow (default Open mode).

1.4 Acceptance Criteria

Criterion Target
cargo test -p rasp-hook 100% pass (≥12 tests)
hook_entry p99 latency (no detection, mock hook) < 500 ns on reference hardware (criterion benchmark)
hook_exit p99 latency < 300 ns
Panic propagation to host 0 (catch_unwind on 100 injected panics)
Registry lookup after freeze zero lock acquisitions (verified by loom or counter)
rasp_hook_entry FFI with null ctx_id returns 0 (Allow), no UB (miri-checked)
All 8 SinkTypes dispatched hook_calls_total counter incremented per sink in smoke test
fail_mode_action() = Allow when FAIL_MODE = Open confirmed by policy-override test

1.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root Cause Debug Signal Recovery
AssertUnwindSafe wrapping non-UnwindSafe data UB or wrong data in catch Arc<Mutex<T>> captured in closure miri error; ASAN report wrap only Copy/Send data; restructure closure to avoid captures
Double-free in FFI arg_ptr heap corruption C caller frees buffer RASP still holds ASAN; use-after-free report Bytes::copy_from_slice immediately; never store raw pointer
SinkType::from_u32 panics on unknown id hook abort FFI sends future sink id current code doesn't know process abort (no catch_unwind at ABI level) from_u32 returns Option; unknown → Allow + warn
Registry lookup race (register concurrent with freeze) data race freeze() called from different thread than register() ThreadSanitizer use Mutex for register; freeze() sets AtomicBool under lock, then drops
on_attach() panic in adapter RASP init fails adapter constructor panics startup log: no "rasp initialized" catch_unwind in RaspGuard::new; log error; RASP fails to init (app proceeds without protection)
hook_exit called without matching hook_entry orphaned exit event adapter fires exit without entry (e.g., exception path) "orphaned_exit" warn log stateless exit handler; no pairing required in Phase 1
FAIL_MODE OnceLock not initialized before first hook call fail_mode_action() panics startup order bug process abort at first hook call RaspGuard::new initializes FAIL_MODE before freezing registry
Trampoline stub returning NotImplemented silently ignored native hooks never fire in Phase 5+ adapter calls install() without checking result no error propagated register_sinks must propagate Err from install()

1.7 Performance Concerns

  • Registry lookup: array-indexed by SinkType as usize → O(1), cache-line-friendly (8 entries fit in 1–2 cache lines); no hash.
  • AtomicBool check: load(Relaxed) ~1 cycle; correct for enable/disable (outcome visible within bounded time).
  • catch_unwind path: ~3–5 ns when no unwind occurs; landing pad setup is compile-time; acceptable on hot path.
  • SinkArgs payload copy in FFI path: Bytes::copy_from_slice for each call; amortize by enforcing arg_len <= 64 KB cap; beyond cap → truncate + warn.
  • hook_exit contention: exits are fire-and-forget; no lock; catch_unwind overhead same as entry.
  • Multi-sink dispatch: single array lookup; no iteration; no branch table beyond enum discriminant.
  • now_ns() via SystemTime: ~30–80 ns syscall (platform); consider std::time::Instant with epoch offset or VDSO-backed clock; leave as SystemTime in Phase 1, optimize Phase 7.
  • Criterion baseline: establish hook_entry_noop benchmark now; Phase 7 must not regress it by > 10%.

1.8 Edge Cases

  • SinkType with u32 value outside [0, SINK_COUNT) in FFI → from_u32 returns None → Allow + "unknown_sink_id" warn.
  • hook_entry called after RASP shutdown (adapter detached) → registry still frozen; hook fires but on_attach already ran on_detach; hook may return Allow or Block depending on state — document as undefined in post-detach period.
  • SinkArgs::Raw with arg_len = 0 → Bytes::copy_from_slice(&[]) → valid empty; analyzer gets empty input.
  • Two adapters registering same SinkType → second registration rejected with Err(SinkAlreadyRegistered) unless explicit override flag passed.
  • hook_exit called from different thread than hook_entry (async runtime moves task) — stateless handler; no per-call state; safe.
  • ctx_id pointer valid but content is all zeros (Uuid::nil()) → logged as "nil_context_id"; no panic.
  • on_detach panic in adapter → catch_unwind in RaspGuard::shutdown; log error; continue shutdown.

1.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Containment Recovery Operator Signal
catch_unwind itself panics (double-panic) process abort process none; OS restarts service fix underlying hook code OS-level restart log; no RASP telemetry
All hooks silently disabled (AtomicBool false) hook_calls_total = 0 for running app all protection silently off health check: rasp_enabled gauge re-enable or redeploy rasp_sinks_enabled_count drops to 0
FFI buffer overflow (arg_len > cap) truncation + warn single event; no heap overflow cap enforced in ABI entry none (truncated args still analyzed) "arg_truncated" warn log + counter
FAIL_MODE init race first hook call panics first N requests blocked OnceLock ensures single init startup ordering fix rasp_init_errors_total
Adapter on_attach failure no sinks registered; all hooks pass-through complete protection loss RASP logs init failure; app unprotected restart RASP "rasp_init_failed" error log
Registry mutation after freeze attempted Err(RegistryFrozen) returned configuration only; no runtime impact error returned to caller dynamic reload deferred to Phase 9 "registry_frozen" warn

1.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: rasp_hook_calls_total{sink, outcome}, rasp_guard_panics_total{sink}, rasp_sinks_enabled_count, rasp_hook_entry_latency_ns histogram.
  • Tracing: tracing::span!("hook_entry", sink=?sink, ctx_id=?ctx_id) wraps dispatch; exportable via OpenTelemetry in Phase 8.
  • Runtime enable/disable: POST /rasp/sinks/{sink}/enable|disable (CLI Phase 9 adds HTTP interface); in Phase 1, toggle via HookRegistration::enabled.store(false, Relaxed).
  • Deployment: rasp-hook is a library; host app links it; no separate process; no IPC.
  • Compatibility: SinkHook trait is #[non_exhaustive] via sealed trait pattern — external crates cannot implement; adapter ABI is versioned via runtime_version().
  • Upgrade path: add new SinkType variants → bump SINK_COUNT; old adapters return None for new sink → Allow (safe degradation).
  • Audit: every hook_entry call logged at trace level; every Block action at warn; every panic at error.

14. Phase 2 — Execution context engine — per-request context store (thread-local + async task-local propagation), HTTP request capture, call stack, correlation id, lifecycle

2.1 Objectives

  • Implement rasp-context fully: RequestContext created at HTTP request boundary, stored in thread-local (thread_local!) for sync adapters AND async task-local (tokio::task_local! or equivalent) for async runtimes; context_id (CorrelationId = Uuid) propagated across sink calls within same request.
  • HttpInfo captured at request ingress: method, URL, headers, body (bounded), source IP, request-id header.
  • CallStack captures Rust call-site frames at each hook entry (bounded depth; no full backtrace on hot path).
  • Lifecycle: ContextGuard RAII type — creates context on new(), clears on drop(). Prevents stale context across request boundaries.
  • Context isolation invariant: concurrent requests NEVER share or leak RequestContext — enforced by thread-local scoping and ContextGuard drop.
  • Constraints: no allocation in CallStack::push on steady state (ring buffer pre-allocated per context); no blocking in context lookup from hook path.

2.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

  • rasp-context/src/context.rs: pub struct RequestContext { pub id: CorrelationId, pub http: HttpInfo, pub taint_map: TaintMap, pub call_stack: CallStack, pub started: Instant }; pub struct HttpInfo { method: CompactStr, url: CompactStr, headers: SmallVec<[(CompactStr, CompactStr); 16]>, body_sample: Bytes, source_ip: Option<IpAddr>, request_id: Option<CompactStr> } (body_sample capped 4 KB, excess truncated + body_truncated: bool; headers capped 64 entries, extras dropped + warn); pub struct CallStack { frames: ArrayVec<CallFrame, 32>, overflow: bool } (arrayvec, no heap on push within capacity); pub struct CallFrame { module: &'static str, function: &'static str, file: &'static str, line: u32 } (all &'static str, zero allocation); pub struct CorrelationId(Uuid) newtype, Display as hyphenated hex.
  • rasp-context/src/store.rs (thread-local store):
thread_local! { static CTX: RefCell<Option<Arc<RequestContext>>> = RefCell::new(None); }

set_context(ctx) → CTX.with(|c| *c.borrow_mut() = Some(ctx)); get_context() -> Option<Arc<RequestContext>> clones the Arc (taint_map has interior mutability via RwLock); clear_context() sets None; with_context<F,R>(f) -> R zero-clone read path for hook dispatch.

  • rasp-context/src/async_store.rs (async task-local, #[cfg(feature = "tokio")]): task_local! { static ASYNC_CTX: Arc<RequestContext>; }; with_async_context(ctx, f) -> F::Output → ASYNC_CTX.scope(ctx, f).await; get_async_context() → ASYNC_CTX.try_with(|c| c.clone()).ok(); falls back to thread-local CTX if unset (sync adapter compatibility).
  • rasp-context/src/guard.rs: pub struct ContextGuard { id: CorrelationId } RAII; ContextGuard::new(http) -> (Self, Arc<RequestContext>) — (1) id = Uuid::new_v4(), (2) construct RequestContext{id, http, taint_map: TaintMap::new(), call_stack: CallStack::new(), started: Instant::now()}, (3) set_context(Arc::clone(&ctx)), (4) return (guard, ctx); impl Drop for ContextGuard { fn drop(&mut self) { clear_context(); } }, infallible. This RAII scoping is the enforcement point for §7 Invariant 7.4 (Context Isolation Invariant) and §6.4 (Context Isolation Per Request), and follows the per-task shadow-state isolation pattern of P14 (Flanagan & Freund, FastTrack).
  • rasp-context/src/lifecycle.rs: request_started(http) -> ContextGuard thin wrapper around ContextGuard::new; request_duration(ctx) -> Duration = ctx.started.elapsed(); telemetry hooks record_request_context_created(ctx_id), record_request_context_cleared(ctx_id, duration).
  • rasp-context/src/callstack.rs: CallStack::push(&mut self, frame) -> bool pushes if frames.len() < 32, sets overflow = true on cap, returns whether pushed; CallStack::current() -> CallFrame uses std::panic::Location::caller() via #[track_caller] (Phase 1 hook entry annotated); populated by hook_entry calling ctx.call_stack.push(CallFrame::current()).
  • rasp-context/src/capture.rs (HTTP capture adapter): pub trait HttpCapture: Send + Sync { fn capture(&self, raw: &dyn Any) -> Result<HttpInfo, RaspError>; }; pub struct MockHttpCapture builds HttpInfo from HashMap<&str, &str> (tests); runtime-specific captures (actix-web, axum, …) are out-of-crate adapters.
  • Integration with rasp-hook: hook_entry calls rasp_context::get_context() (or async variant) to resolve ctx_id; if None, generates ephemeral Uuid::new_v4() + logs "no_request_context"; RaspGuard::on_entry receives HookEvent with resolved context, passes Arc<RequestContext> to downstream phases.
  • rasp-context/src/taint_map.rs (Phase 2 stub): pub struct TaintMap { inner: HashMap<String, TaintedValue> } keyed by parameter name; insert(&mut self, key, val), get(&self, key) -> Option<&TaintedValue>, is_empty(&self) -> bool; actual propagation lands in Phase 3 (rasp-taint) — TaintMap here is the shared store.
  • Tests: rasp-context/tests/lifecycle_test.rs (ContextGuard create/drop, thread-local cleared after drop); rasp-context/tests/isolation_test.rs (2 threads each create a guard, assert IDs differ and never cross-visible); rasp-context/tests/callstack_test.rs (push 32 frames at cap, push 33rd → overflow=true, no panic); rasp-context/tests/http_capture_test.rs (MockHttpCapture with headers, body > 4 KB → truncation); rasp-context/tests/async_propagation_test.rs (tokio spawn with with_async_context, inner task reads context, assert same id).

2.3 Deliverable

  • rasp-context compiles with tokio feature; all tests pass.
  • ContextGuard lifecycle verified: create → readable from hook → drop → clear → hook sees None.
  • Cross-thread isolation demonstrated: 100-thread stress test, zero leaked context IDs.
  • CallStack pushes 32 frames without allocation; overflow flag set on 33rd.
  • MockHttpCapture produces HttpInfo with body truncation at 4 KB.
  • Async propagation: with_async_context + tokio::spawn sub-task reads correct context.

2.4 Acceptance Criteria

Criterion Target
cargo test -p rasp-context 100% pass (≥15 tests)
get_context() latency (thread-local read) < 50 ns p99 (criterion)
ContextGuard::drop() clears context 100% in 100-thread leak test (ThreadSanitizer)
CallStack::push for 32 frames 0 heap allocations (DHAT / #[global_allocator] counting)
Body truncation at 4 KB body_truncated = true, body_sample.len() = 4096
Async propagation across tokio::spawn context readable in spawned task (10k iteration test)
Ephemeral context on missing guard "no_request_context" warn logged; no panic
rasp_context_created_total counter incremented once per ContextGuard::new
